```json
      {
        "tag_code": "HIGH_MYOPIA",
        "tag_name": "高度近视",
        "tag_group": "CLINICAL",
        "tag_source": "AUTO",
        "confidence": 1.000,
        "effective_from": "2026-08-12"
      }
    ],
    "addresses": [
      {
        "id": 881,
        "address_type": "DELIVERY",
        "receiver_name": "王女士",
        "receiver_phone_mask": "138****1234",
        "full_address": "北京市朝阳区建国路 88 号 3 号楼 502",
        "is_default": true,
        "delivery_preference": "EXPRESS"
      }
    ],
    "ext": {
      "occupation": "小学教师",
      "occupation_class": "INDOOR",
      "usage_scene": ["STUDY", "OFFICE", "DRIVING"],
      "primary_scene": "DRIVING",
      "driving_need": "BOTH",
      "night_driving_freq": "FREQUENT",
      "face_shape": "ROUND",
      "face_width_level": "NORMAL",
      "skin_sensitive": true,
      "is_high_myopia": true,
      "high_myopia_eye": "OU",
      "myopia_degree_ou": -8.50
    },
    "created_at": "2024-05-06T10:12:00+08:00",
    "updated_at": "2026-10-28T15:20:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 按 `customer_no` 查主表；不存在或 `is_deleted` 返回 `"1005001"`。
2. `include` 决定是否联查扩展/标签/地址（缺省全部包含）。
3. **手机号明文只在请求方具备 `m10:customer:sensitive` 权限时返回 `phone` 字段**，否则只返回 `phone_mask`。
4. 读取明文手机号/身份证时写一条 `VIEW_SENSITIVE` 日志。
5. 此接口是**页面用**详情，与内部契约 P-10（API-39）的区别：P-10 不下发明文敏感信息且返回字段更精简。

#### API-08 手机号查重

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/customers/check-phone?phone=13800001234
```

**响应（无重复）**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "exists": false,
    "existing_customer_no": null,
    "existing_customer_name": null,
    "candidate_patients": []
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**响应（已有顾客）**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "exists": true,
    "existing_customer_no": "KH000128",
    "existing_customer_name": "王女士",
    "existing_status": "ACTIVE",
    "last_visit_at": "2026-10-28T15:20:00+08:00",
    "candidate_patients": [
      {
        "patient_id": 10021,
        "patient_name": "王丽",
        "gender": "F",
        "age": 32,
        "match_score": 0.88,
        "match_basis": ["PHONE_EXACT"]
      }
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验手机号格式，非法返回 `"1005003"`。
2. 计算 `phone_hash`（SHA-256 + 盐）后走唯一索引 `uk_m10_customer_phone` 查询。
3. `candidate_patients` 异步调 M03-01 按手机号匹配患者，供建档时提示「是否关联」；M03 不可用时该字段返回空数组（降级）。
4. 该接口只读，不写日志。

#### API-09 新建顾客

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/customers
Idempotency-Key: 7f3a9b2c-1d4e-8f6a-0b5c-3d7e9f1a2b6c
```

```json
{
  "customer_name": "王女士",
  "phone": "13800001234",
  "gender": "F",
  "birth_date": "1994-03-12",
  "source": "WALK_IN",
  "customer_type": "ADULT",
  "allergy_history": "金属镜架过敏",
  "medical_history": "糖尿病10年，双眼糖网3期",
  "consent_marketing": true,
  "store_code": "SC001",
  "ext": {
    "occupation": "小学教师",
    "occupation_class": "INDOOR",
    "usage_scene": ["STUDY", "OFFICE", "DRIVING"],
    "primary_scene": "DRIVING",
    "driving_need": "BOTH",
    "night_driving_freq": "FREQUENT",
    "face_shape": "ROUND",
    "skin_sensitive": true
  },
  "tag_codes": ["VIP"],
  "address": {
    "address_type": "DELIVERY",
    "receiver_name": "王女士",
    "receiver_phone": "13800001234",
    "province_code": "110000",
    "city_code": "110100",
    "district_code": "110105",
    "detail_address": "建国路 88 号 3 号楼 502",
    "is_default": true,
    "delivery_preference": "EXPRESS"
  }
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "customer_no": "KH000128",
    "customer_name": "王女士",
    "link_status": "UNLINKED",
    "patient_id": null,
    "tags": ["VIP"],
    "candidate_patients": [
      {
        "patient_id": 10021,
        "patient_name": "王丽",
        "match_score": 0.88,
        "suggest_link": true
      }
    ],
    "created_at": "2026-10-31T10:12:03+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验参数：姓名、手机号格式、身份证校验位（`"1005003"`/`"1005004"`）。
2. 手机号查重（`phone_hash` 唯一索引）：命中活跃顾客 → `"1005002"` 并返回已有顾客号。
3. 身份证查重（非空时）：命中 → `"1005005"`。
4. 来源校验：`REFERRAL`/`MEMBER_REFERRAL` 必须填 `referrer_customer_no`，否则 `"1005006"`。
5. 生成顾客号：`INCR m10:code:KH` → `'KH' + str(seq).zfill(6)`。
6. 计算 `phone_hash`、`name_pinyin`、`name_pinyin_short`、`age`、`age_group`。
7. 事务内写入 `m10_customer` + `m10_customer_ext` + `m10_customer_tag`（手工标签）+ `m10_customer_address`。
8. 写日志 `CREATE`（`action_group=CUSTOMER`）。
9. 异步调 M03-01 按手机号匹配患者，命中则返回 `candidate_patients` 供前台提示关联；**不自动回填 `patient_id`**。
10. 发布事件 `m10.customer.created`。
11. 支持 `Idempotency-Key`（Redis `m10:idem:customer:create:{key}` TTL 24h）。

#### API-10 更新顾客（非敏感字段）

**请求**

```
PUT /api/v1/m10/customers/KH000128
```

```json
{
  "customer_name": "王女士",
  "gender": "F",
  "allergy_history": "金属镜架过敏；护理液过敏",
  "medical_history": "糖尿病10年，双眼糖网3期",
  "consent_marketing": true,
  "remark": "老顾客，习惯中高端镜架",
  "version": 5
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "customer_no": "KH000128",
    "version": 6,
    "changed_fields": ["allergy_history", "remark"],
    "updated_at": "2026-10-31T10:20:11+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验 `version` 乐观锁，不匹配返回 `"1005010"`。
2. **手机号与身份证不允许通过本接口修改**（`extra="forbid"`，请求体含这两个字段直接 40001），必须走 API-11。
3. 顾客状态为 `MERGED` 时拒绝（`"1005007"`）。
4. 比对变更字段，写日志 `UPDATE`（含 `before_snapshot` / `after_snapshot`，敏感字段脱敏）。
5. `consent_marketing` 由 false → true 时记录 `consent_at`。

#### API-11 变更手机号（敏感，需审批）

**请求**

```
PATCH /api/v1/m10/customers/KH000128/phone
```

```json
{
  "new_phone": "13900005678",
  "change_reason": "顾客换号，本人到店口头告知并出示身份证",
  "approval_no": "AP202610310012",
  "verify_code": "864213"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "customer_no": "KH000128",
    "phone_mask": "139****5678",
    "changed_at": "2026-10-31T10:25:40+08:00",
    "log_no": "CL2026103100012834"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验新手机号格式（`"1005003"`）与新手机号未被占用（`"1005002"`）。
2. 校验 `change_reason` 长度 ≥ 4 且 `approval_no` 非空，否则 `"1005011"`。
3. 审批单号校验：调 M20 校验审批单有效且已通过（不可用时降级为「记录待补验」）。
4. 更新 `phone`、`phone_hash`。
5. 写日志 `PHONE_CHANGE`，`is_sensitive = TRUE`、`approval_no`、`change_reason`、脱敏前后快照。
6. 同一顾客 30 天内最多变更 2 次，超限需店长二次审批。

#### API-12 停用/启用/黑名单

**请求**

```
PATCH /api/v1/m10/customers/KH000128/status
```

```json
{
  "target_status": "DISABLED",
  "reason": "顾客申请注销档案",
  "approval_no": null
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "customer_no": "KH000128",
    "old_status": "ACTIVE",
    "new_status": "DISABLED",
    "updated_at": "2026-10-31T10:30:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 允许的目标状态：`ACTIVE`（启用）、`DISABLED`（停用）、`BLACKLIST`（黑名单）。
2. 转 `BLACKLIST` 必须有 `approval_no` 与原因。
3. 目标为 `ACTIVE` 时校验手机号唯一性（若期间该手机号被他人占用则 `"1005002"`）。
4. 有进行中会话时不允许停用（提示先结束会话）。
5. 写日志（`DISABLE`/`ENABLE`/`BLACKLIST`/`UNBLACKLIST`）。

#### API-13 回填 patient_id（单向关联）

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/customers/KH000128/link-patient
```

```json
{
  "patient_id": 10021,
  "link_source": "REGISTRATION",
  "verify_phone": true,
  "reason": "顾客 2026-10-31 到院挂号就诊，与本院患者档案为同一人"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "customer_no": "KH000128",
    "patient_id": 10021,
    "empi_no": "P202610200001",
    "link_status": "LINKED",
    "link_source": "REGISTRATION",
    "phone_verified": true,
    "linked_at": "2026-10-31T10:35:12+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 顾客当前 `link_status` 必须为 `UNLINKED`/`PENDING`/`CONFLICT`；已 `LINKED` 返回 `"1005007"`（不可重复回填）。
2. `verify_phone = true` 时调 M03-01 `patient/{id}/brief` 取患者手机号，与顾客手机号比对；不一致 → `"1005040"`。
3. 校验该 `patient_id` 是否已被其他顾客关联（`idx_m10_customer_patient`）；若已关联另一 `ACTIVE` 顾客 → `"1005040"` 并返回冲突方顾客号。
4. 更新 `patient_id`、`patient_no`、`empi_no`、`link_status='LINKED'`、`linked_at`、`linked_by`、`link_source`。
5. 写日志 `LINK_PATIENT`，`is_sensitive = TRUE`（涉及患者身份关联）。
6. **绝不写 M03 的任何表**——本接口只更新 `m10_customer`。
7. 回填成功后触发一次历史选配/订单的 `patient_id` 关联刷新（异步）。

#### API-14 顾客档案合并

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/customers/merge
```

```json
{
  "source_customer_nos": ["KH000341", "KH000512"],
  "target_customer_no": "KH000128",
  "merge_reason": "同一顾客重复建档，手机号与身份证一致",
  "approval_no": "AP202610310015"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "target_customer_no": "KH000128",
    "merged_sources": [
      {"customer_no": "KH000341", "moved_sessions": 2, "moved_tags": 3},
      {"customer_no": "KH000512", "moved_sessions": 0, "moved_tags": 1}
    ],
    "target_version": 7,
    "merged_at": "2026-10-31T10:40:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验目标不能出现在来源中（`"1005029"`）、来源数 ≤ 10（`"1005031"`）。
2. 目标与所有来源必须存在且状态为 `ACTIVE`（`"1005030"`）。
3. **禁止合并规则**：若来源与目标关联的 `patient_id` 不同且都非空 → 拒绝 `"1005040"`（可能是不同的人）。
4. 事务内执行：
   - `m10_selection_session.customer_id/customer_no` 重定向到目标；
   - `m10_customer_tag` 迁移（目标已有同标签则来源标签置失效）；
   - `m10_customer_address` 迁移（默认地址冲突时来源默认置 false）；
   - `m10_customer_ext` 合并（目标为空的字段用来源填充）；
   - `m10_customer_log.customer_id` **不迁移**（日志保留原始归属，另写 MERGE 日志）；
   - 来源置 `customer_status='MERGED'`、`merged_into_no=目标号`。
5. 写两条日志：`MERGE_SOURCE`（每个来源一条）、`MERGE_TARGET`（目标一条），均 `is_sensitive = TRUE`。
6. 发布事件 `m10.customer.merged`（M10-06 / M10-08 消费并重定向）。

#### API-15 变更历史

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/customers/KH000128/logs?action=ALL&page=1&page_size=20
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [
      {
        "log_no": "CL2026103100012834",
        "action": "PHONE_CHANGE",
        "action_group": "CUSTOMER",
        "change_summary": "手机号由 138****1234 变更为 139****5678",
        "change_reason": "顾客换号，本人到店口头告知",
        "is_sensitive": true,
        "approval_no": "AP202610310012",
        "operator_name": "李四",
        "operator_role": "FRONT_DESK",
        "result": "SUCCESS",
        "created_at": "2026-10-31T10:25:40+08:00"
      }
    ],
    "total": 18,
    "page": 1,
    "page_size": 20,
    "pages": 1
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 查 `m10_customer_log` 按 `customer_id` + `created_at DESC`，走 `idx_m10_customer_log_cust`。
2. 查询窗口缺省近 36 个月；`action` 支持按动作过滤。
3. 敏感日志（`is_sensitive = TRUE`）需 `m10:customer:audit` 权限，否则过滤掉并在响应中标注 `filtered_sensitive_count`。
4. `before_snapshot`/`after_snapshot` 只在具备审计权限且显式传 `include_snapshot=true` 时返回。

#### API-17 视光画像保存

**请求**

```
PUT /api/v1/m10/customers/KH000128/ext
```

```json
{
  "occupation": "小学教师",
  "occupation_class": "INDOOR",
  "usage_scene": ["STUDY", "OFFICE", "DRIVING"],
  "primary_scene": "DRIVING",
  "daily_screen_hours": 8.5,
  "driving_need": "BOTH",
  "night_driving_freq": "FREQUENT",
  "face_shape": "ROUND",
  "skin_sensitive": true,
  "previous_concerns": "镜片太重、镜架易下滑",
  "preferred_brand": ["ESSILOR", "ZEISS"],
  "ext_version": 3
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "customer_no": "KH000128",
    "ext_version": 4,
    "changed_fields": ["primary_scene", "face_shape"],
    "updated_at": "2026-10-31T10:45:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. `primary_scene` 必须在 `usage_scene` 内，否则 `"1005032"`。
2. `driving_need` 为 `NIGHT`/`BOTH` 时 `night_driving_freq` 必填，否则 `"1005033"`。
3. 列表字段（`usage_scene`/`preferred_brand`/`lens_pref_coating`）逗号拼接后入库。
4. `ext_version` 乐观锁冲突返回 `"1005010"`。
5. 写日志 `UPDATE`（`action_group=CUSTOMER`，`changed_fields` 记录 `ext.*`）。

#### API-18 打开选配会话

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/customers/selection-sessions
Idempotency-Key: 3c8e1f4a-9b2d-4e6f-8a1c-5d9e3f7b2a4c
```

```json
{
  "customer_no": "KH000128",
  "session_type": "REPURCHASE",
  "rx_no": "RX202610310012",
  "optometry_no": "YG202610310005",
  "store_code": "SC001",
  "device_id": "PAD-03"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "session_no": "SX202610310021",
    "customer_no": "KH000128",
    "session_status": "OPEN",
    "session_type": "REPURCHASE",
    "salesperson_id": 5001,
    "salesperson_name": "张三",
    "rx_no": "RX202610310012",
    "rx_snapshot": {
      "od": {"sphere": -8.50, "cylinder": -0.75, "axis": 180},
      "os": {"sphere": -8.25, "cylinder": -0.50, "axis": 175},
      "pd": 64.00,
      "valid_until": "2026-11-30"
    },
    "session_ttl_at": "2026-10-31T14:45:00+08:00",
    "resumed": false,
    "opened_at": "2026-10-31T10:45:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验顾客存在且 `customer_status = 'ACTIVE'`；`MERGED` 返回 `"1005019"` 并给出目标顾客号。
2. **幂等恢复**：若该顾客已有 `OPEN`/`QUOTED` 会话且未过期 → 直接返回该会话（`resumed = true`），不新建。
3. 校验当前销售员并发会话数 < 3，否则 `"1005023"`。
4. 生成会话号 `SX` + `YYYYMMDD` + 4 位（Redis `m10:code:SX:{yyyyMMdd}`）。
5. 调 M10-02 P-05 取处方快照；无 `rx_no` 时调 P-06 取患者有效处方（`patient_id` 非空时）。
6. 调 M10-01 P-01 取验光快照（PD/瞳高）。
7. 落库 `m10_selection_session`，写 Redis `m10:selection:{customer_no}`（TTL 4h）。
8. 写日志 `SESSION_OPEN`（`action_group=SESSION`）。

#### API-19 候选商品（处方驱动 + 光度预校验）

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/customers/selection-sessions/SX202610310021/candidates
    ?item_role=FRAME&sort_by=face_match&page=1&page_size=20&prelookup=true
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [
      {
        "product_code": "OPFR000123",
        "product_name": "雷朋 RB2140 板材镜架",
        "brand_name": "雷朋",
        "item_role": "FRAME",
        "spec_text": "52-18-145",
        "image_url": "/upload/optical/OPFR000123_1.jpg",
        "frame_width": 52,
        "bridge_width": 18,
        "temple_length": 145,
        "frame_material": "ACETATE",
        "face_match_score": 92.00,
        "pd_match_level": "GOOD",
        "match_detail": {
          "face_score": 1.0,
          "width_score": 1.0,
          "pd_score": 0.6,
          "material_score": 1.0
        },
        "final_price": 680.00,
        "price_source": "MEMBER",
        "supply_type": "STOCK",
        "lead_time_days": 0,
        "lookup_status": "AVAILABLE"
      },
      {
        "product_code": "OPLN000457",
        "product_name": "依视路 钻晶A4 1.67 非球面",
        "brand_name": "依视路",
        "item_role": "LENS",
        "refractive_index": "1.67",
        "lens_design": "ASPHERIC",
        "image_url": "/upload/optical/OPLN000457_1.jpg",
        "recommended_ri": true,
        "final_price": 622.00,
        "surcharge": 50.00,
        "supply_type": "CUSTOM",
        "lead_time_days": 7,
        "lookup_status": "CUSTOM"
      }
    ],
    "total": 128,
    "page": 1,
    "page_size": 20,
    "pages": 7,
    "lookup_batch_ref": "bl_20261031_104512",
    "price_source": "M01-08 pricing"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. **候选池筛选**：调 M01-08 `GET /internal/v1/optical/products/eligible`，条件含 `item_role` 对应品类、`status=ENABLED`、`is_trial_lens=false`。
2. **处方驱动过滤**（镜片类）：根据处方度数计算推荐折射率（3.4（2）），把非推荐的折射率置于结果后段并标注 `recommended_ri=false`。
3. **镜架匹配度**：按 3.4（1）计算 `face_match_score`、`pd_match_level`、`match_detail`。
4. **光度预校验**（`prelookup=true` 时）：对当页商品批量调 M01-08 `POST /internal/v1/optical/specs/batch-lookup`，把 `is_available`/`supply_type` 映射为 `lookup_status`。
5. **取价**：对当页商品批量调 M01-08 `GET .../{code}/pricing`，取 `final_price`/`surcharge`/`price_source`。**本模块不参与运算。**
6. 图片地址来自 M01-08 `mdm_optical_image`（`is_primary = true`）。
7. 结果缓存 Redis `m10:customer:candidates:{session_no}:{item_role}:{page}` TTL 300s。
8. 排序：`face_match`（匹配度降序）/`price_asc`/`price_desc`/`sales_desc`/`newest`。

3. 会话已过期（`session_ttl_at < now()` 且状态仍为 `OPEN`/`QUOTED`）时先异步触发过期，返回 `"1005013"`。
4. 金额字段全部为会话上的快照（来自上游），原样返回。

#### API-21 会话保活/延长

**请求**

```
PATCH /api/v1/m10/customers/selection-sessions/SX202610310021/heartbeat
```

```json
{
  "extend_minutes": 120
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "session_no": "SX202610310021",
    "session_ttl_at": "2026-10-31T16:45:00+08:00",
    "extended_minutes": 120,
    "extend_count": 1
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验会话存在且状态为 `OPEN`/`QUOTED`，否则 `"1005013"`/`"1005014"`。
2. `extend_minutes` 取值 30/60/120，单会话累计延长不超过 240 分钟（超过需重新打开）。
3. 更新 `last_active_at`、`session_ttl_at`；同步刷新 Redis TTL。
4. 不写日志（高频操作，避免日志膨胀）。

#### API-22 换人接待

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/customers/selection-sessions/SX202610310021/handover
```

```json
{
  "new_salesperson_id": 5008,
  "reason": "原销售员被叫去协助他人试戴，转交同事继续接待",
  "version": 4
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "session_no": "SX202610310021",
    "old_salesperson_id": 5001,
    "old_salesperson_name": "张三",
    "new_salesperson_id": 5008,
    "new_salesperson_name": "王芳",
    "handover_at": "2026-10-31T10:52:00+08:00",
    "drafts_preserved": 3,
    "version": 5
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验会话状态为 `OPEN`/`QUOTED`，否则 `"1005014"`。
2. 校验新销售员存在且具备 `m10:customer:select` 权限。
3. `version` 乐观锁校验，冲突 `"1005010"`。
4. 若 `original_salesperson_id` 为空，先写入当前 `salesperson_id`。
5. 更新 `salesperson_id`、`salesperson_name`，`handler_log` 追加 `{from, to, at, reason}`。
6. **草稿与会话号完全保留**（这是本设计的关键收益）。
7. 写日志 `SESSION_HANDOVER`。
8. 向原销售员推送 WebSocket 提醒「会话已转交」。

#### API-23 加入选配草稿行

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/customers/selection-sessions/SX202610310021/drafts
Idempotency-Key: 8d2f5a1c-4e7b-9a3d-6c1f-2b8e4d7a9c5f
```

```json
{
  "product_code": "OPLN000457",
  "item_role": "LENS",
  "quantity": 2,
  "eye_side": "OU",
  "sphere": -8.50,
  "cylinder": -0.75,
  "axis": 180,
  "add_power": null,
  "pupil_distance": 64.00,
  "pupil_height": 24.00,
  "sales_note": "顾客偏好轻薄，接受定制 7 天"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "session_no": "SX202610310021",
    "line_no": 2,
    "product_code": "OPLN000457",
    "product_name": "依视路 钻晶A4 1.67 非球面",
    "lookup_status": "CUSTOM",
    "supply_type": "CUSTOM",
    "lead_time_days": 7,
    "final_price": 622.00,
    "surcharge": 50.00,
    "base_price": 572.00,
    "price_source": "MEMBER",
    "item_count": 2,
    "session_version": 5
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验会话状态 `OPEN`/`QUOTED`，否则 `"1005014"`。
2. 校验商品：调 M01-08 `full-config`，商品不存在/停用/试戴片 → `"1005024"`。
3. 度数规则校验：镜片必须有球镜（`"1005026"`）；球柱镜 0.25 步进、轴位与柱镜联动（`"1005015"`）。
4. 需验配商品（隐形 `CONTACT`/OK镜 `ORTHO_K`）→ 检查是否已有 `contact_fit_no`/`ortho_k_fit_no`，无则 `"1005027"` 并返回验配跳转地址。
5. 生成 `line_no`：`SELECT max(line_no) FOR UPDATE` + 1（会话级锁，避免并发重号）。
6. **立即触发光度校验与取价**：调 M01-08 `specs/lookup` → `pricing`，结果落 `lookup_result`/`pricing_snapshot`，并写关键列。
7. 若 `lookup_status = 'UNAVAILABLE'`，同步调替换推荐算法（3.5）并落 `replacement_json`。
8. 更新会话 `item_count`、`candidate_count`、`unavailable_count`。
9. 同步写 Redis 热态草稿。
10. 若会话状态为 `QUOTED`，置为 `OPEN`（草稿已变更，报价失效）。

#### API-24 修改草稿行

**请求**

```
PUT /api/v1/m10/customers/selection-sessions/SX202610310021/drafts/2
```

```json
{
  "quantity": 2,
  "is_selected": true,
  "sales_note": "顾客确认定制",
  "sort_no": 100,
  "version": 1
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "session_no": "SX202610310021",
    "line_no": 2,
    "version": 2,
    "item_count": 2,
    "quote_invalidated": true,
    "updated_at": "2026-10-31T11:02:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验会话与草稿行存在（`"1005013"`/`"1005036"`）。
2. 乐观锁 `version` 校验（`"1005010"`）。
3. 数量变更时重算该行金额展示（调 `pricing` 取单价，金额聚合由 API-26 负责）。
4. 草稿变更后若会话为 `QUOTED`，置回 `OPEN` 并返回 `quote_invalidated=true`，提示「需重新报价」。
5. 更新 Redis 热态。

#### API-25 移除草稿行（逻辑）

**请求**

```
DELETE /api/v1/m10/customers/selection-sessions/SX202610310021/drafts/3?reason=顾客放弃该镜片
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "session_no": "SX202610310021",
    "line_no": 3,
    "draft_status": "REMOVED",
    "item_count": 2,
    "quote_invalidated": true
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验会话状态为 `OPEN`/`QUOTED`（`"1005014"`）。
2. 不物理删除：`draft_status = 'REMOVED'`，保留试戴与放弃记录（用于 P08「试戴转化率」分析）。
3. 更新会话 `item_count`；会话置回 `OPEN`。
4. 行号**不重排**（保持审计可追溯）。

#### API-26 触发光度校验并取价

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/customers/selection-sessions/SX202610310021/drafts/lookup
```

```json
{
  "line_nos": [2],
  "force_refresh": false
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "results": [
      {
        "line_no": 2,
        "product_code": "OPLN000457",
        "lookup_status": "CUSTOM",
        "supply_type": "CUSTOM",
        "lead_time_days": 7,
        "base_price": 572.00,
        "surcharge": 50.00,
        "final_price": 622.00,
        "floor_price": 457.60,
        "price_source": "MEMBER",
        "message": "该度数需定制，预计 7 天供货"
      }
    ],
    "session_summary": {
      "gross_amount": 2004.00,
      "frame_amount": 680.00,
      "lens_amount": 1244.00,
      "surcharge_amount": 100.00,
      "service_amount": 80.00,
      "item_count": 3,
      "unavailable_count": 0
    },
    "spec_version": "v12:20261031-083000"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 逐行调 M01-08 `POST /internal/v1/optical/specs/lookup`（≤3 行时逐条，>3 行时用 `batch-lookup`）。
2. 逐行调 M01-08 `GET /internal/v1/optical/products/{code}/pricing` 取 `final_price`/`surcharge`/`floor_price`。
3. 记录 `lookup_spec_version`（M01-08 `/version` 返回）；版本变化时强制失效重校验。
4. **`session_summary` 的各分项金额**：`frame_amount`/`lens_amount`/`surcharge_amount` 由各行的 `final_price`/`surcharge` **按行加总得到**——这里的加总是「把上游返回值汇总成页面展示的小计」，属于展示层聚合，**不是定价计算**；实际收银金额由 M16-01 引擎负责。
5. 更新会话的汇总字段与草稿行。
6. 上游不可用时：`"1005008"`（lookup）/`"1005009"`（pricing），并保留上一次成功结果标注「可能过期」。

#### API-27 放弃/取消会话

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/customers/selection-sessions/SX202610310021/abandon
```

```json
{
  "abandon_reason_code": "PRICE",
  "close_reason": "顾客认为价格超出预算，决定再考虑"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "session_no": "SX202610310021",
    "session_status": "ABANDONED",
    "abandon_reason_code": "PRICE",
    "closed_at": "2026-10-31T11:10:00+08:00",
    "item_count": 3,
    "payable_amount": 1703.60
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 会话状态必须为 `OPEN`/`QUOTED`，否则 `"1005014"`。
2. `abandon_reason_code` 必填且必须在枚举内；`close_reason` ≥ 2 字。
3. 更新 `session_status='ABANDONED'`、`closed_at`、`abandon_reason_code`、`close_reason`。
4. 删除 Redis 热态键 `m10:selection:{customer_no}`。
5. 写日志 `SESSION_ABANDON`。
6. 发布事件 `m10.customer.selection.abandoned`（供 P08 转化率与商品结构分析）。
7. **草稿行不删除**——已放弃会话的商品选择是「需求但未满足」的黄金数据。

#### API-28 光度校验详情

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/customers/selection-sessions/SX202610310021/drafts/2/lookup-detail
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "session_no": "SX202610310021",
    "line_no": 2,
    "product_code": "OPLN000456",
    "product_name": "依视路 钻晶A4 1.60 非球面",
    "required": {
      "od": {"sphere": -8.50, "cylinder": -0.75, "axis": 180},
      "os": {"sphere": -8.25, "cylinder": -0.50, "axis": 175}
    },
    "lookup_status": "UNAVAILABLE",
    "is_available": false,
    "supply_type": null,
    "message": "该度数组合（球镜 -8.50 / 柱镜 -0.75）超出可供应范围，请更换产品",
    "available_range": {
      "sphere_range": "-8.00 ~ +6.00",
      "cylinder_range": "-2.00 ~ 0.00"
    },
    "gap_analysis": [
      {
        "eye_side": "OD",
        "dimension": "SPHERE",
        "required_value": -8.50,
        "boundary_value": -8.00,
        "gap": 0.50
      },
      {
        "eye_side": "OS",
        "dimension": "SPHERE",
        "required_value": -8.25,
        "boundary_value": -8.00,
        "gap": 0.25
      }
    ],
    "lookup_at": "2026-10-31T10:58:00+08:00",
    "spec_version": "v12:20261031-083000",
    "customer_hint": "顾客标签为「高度近视 + 糖网配镜」，建议优先选择 1.67 及以上折射率"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 取草稿行的 `lookup_result` 快照；若 `lookup_spec_version` 落后于当前主数据版本，先重新校验。
2. `available_range` 调 M01-08 `full-config` 的 `spec_summary` 获取。
3. `gap_analysis`：本模块用 `required_value − boundary_value` 计算度数缺口（**这是视觉解释，不是金额计算**）。
4. `customer_hint`：按顾客标签与画像生成选品建议文案。
5. 若为 `AVAILABLE`/`CUSTOM` 状态则 `gap_analysis` 为空数组。

#### API-29 替换推荐计算

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/customers/selection-sessions/SX202610310021/drafts/2/replacements
```

```json
{
  "max_candidates": 8,
  "strategies": ["SAME_BRAND_SAME_RI", "NEIGHBOR_DEGREE", "HIGHER_RI", "CUSTOM_ORDER", "OTHER_BRAND"]
}
```

**响应**

```json

      },
      {

      },
      {
        "strategy": "CUSTOM_ORDER",

        "product_code": "OPLN000456",
        "product_name": "依视路 钻晶A4 1.60 非球面（车房定制）",
        "brand_name": "依视路",
        "refractive_index": "1.60",
        "supply_type": "CUSTOM",
        "lead_time_days": 7,
        "final_price": null,
        "price_delta": null,
        "cover_degree": true,
        "recommend_level": "FALLBACK",
        "reason": "按此参数车房定制，需 7 天，预计到货 2026-11-07"
      }
    ],
    "elapsed_ms": 386
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑（完整实现 3.5 节算法）：**

1. 取草稿行的度数（OD/OS 分别校验）与原商品的品牌、折射率、镜片设计、膜层。
2. **策略 ①同品牌同折射率**：调 M01-08 `eligible` 带 `brand_code` + `refractive_index` + `lens_design`；逐候选调 `specs/lookup`（>3 个时 `batch-lookup`）；命中 `is_available = true` 的取价。
3. **策略 ②邻近度数**：对每个候选商品，把顾客度数按 0.25D 步进在容差范围内浮动，判断是否存在可供格；`cover_degree` 取「该商品是否能在容差内覆盖该度数」，并在 `reason` 中说明替代度数。
4. **策略 ③更高折射率**：按 `1.56→1.60→1.67→1.74` 阶梯取下一档，重复策略 ①的候选筛选与校验。
5. **策略 ④定制**：若原商品本身支持车房定制（`full-config` 中 `is_lab_lens = true`），调 `pricing` 取 `lead_time_days`，`price_delta = null`（定制价以 M16 下单时为准）。
6. **策略 ⑤跨品牌兜底**：调 `eligible` 带 `refractive_index` + `lens_design`（不限品牌），排序同价位段优先。
7. 汇总去重，按 `recommend_level` → `strategy` → `lead_time_days` → `|price_delta|` 升序排序，截取 `max_candidates`。
8. `best_strategy` 取首个 PRIMARY 候选的策略；若无 PRIMARY 则取首个 ALTERNATIVE。
9. **全部策略均无命中** → `"1005035"`（四级全空，理论上极罕见：意味着该度数与任何镜片都不匹配）。
10. 结果写 `replacement_json`，`replace_status = 'SUGGESTED'`。
11. 结果缓存 Redis `m10:customer:replace:{session_no}:{line_no}` TTL 600s。
12. 单次耗时预算 ≤ 1.2s（并行调用 eligible + lookup）。

#### API-30 接受替换建议

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/customers/selection-sessions/SX202610310021/drafts/2/replace
Idempotency-Key: 5e9b2c7d-1a4f-8e3b-6d2c-9f1a4b7e3c8d
```

```json
{
  "line_no": 2,
  "new_product_code": "OPLN000457",
  "reason": "顾客同意升级 1.67 折射率换取可做与更薄",
  "keep_degree": true
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "session_no": "SX202610310021",
    "replaced_line_no": 2,
    "new_line_no": 4,
    "new_product_code": "OPLN000457",
    "new_product_name": "依视路 钻晶A4 1.67 非球面",
    "old_draft_status": "REMOVED",
    "new_lookup_status": "CUSTOM",
    "new_final_price": 622.00,
    "new_lead_time_days": 7,
    "item_count": 3,
    "unavailable_count": 0
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验会话与草稿行（`"1005013"`/`"1005036"`）。
2. 校验原行确为不可供（`lookup_status IN ('UNAVAILABLE','ERROR')`），否则 `"1005034"`。
3. 校验新商品在替换候选清单内（防止前端篡改自由换货；人工换货须走 `replace_status='MANUAL'` 的另一个入口）。
4. 创建新草稿行（`line_no = max + 1`），沿用原度数（`keep_degree=true`）并立即做光度校验与取价。
5. 新行 `replaced_from_code`、`replaced_from_line_no`、`replace_reason` 回填。
6. 原行置 `draft_status='REMOVED'`、`replace_status='ACCEPTED'`。
7. 更新会话计数与时间戳，写日志。

```json
    },

  },

```

**业务逻辑（严格遵守「不自行计算金额」）：**

1. 校验会话状态 `OPEN`/`QUOTED`（`"1005013"`/`"1005014"`）；草稿至少 1 行勾选（`"1005018"`）。
2. 校验无 `UNAVAILABLE` 行（`"1005021"`）；需验配商品已验配（`"1005027"`）。
3. 对每个勾选行调 M01-08 `pricing` 取 `final_price`/`surcharge`（若草稿快照版本落后则刷新）。
4. **调 M16-06 `GET /internal/v1/m16/discount/available`** 取可用优惠候选（`patient_id` 为空时按顾客号与会员等级传参）。
5. **调 M16-06 `POST /internal/v1/m16/discount/calc`** 计算优惠组合与应付金额。请求体中包含各行的商品编码、数量、单价（来自 M01-08）、会员等级、指定 `policy_codes`。
6. **调 M10-08 P-22** `GET /internal/v1/m10/members/benefits/by-patient/{patient_id}` 取套餐剩余次数（`patient_id` 为空时跳过并置 `benefit_remain_times = null`）。
7. 生成报价单号 `BJ` + `YYYYMMDD` + 4 位。
8. 落会话快照：`quote_no`、`quote_snapshot`（完整响应）、`gross_amount`/`discount_amount`/`payable_amount`（**全部为上游返回值**）、`quoted_at`、`quote_expire_at`（+2h）、`session_status='QUOTED'`。
9. **不落账、不生成结算单、不写 M16 任何表**。
10. `warnings`：定制片到货提示、价格接近 `floor_price` 的提示（`final_price - floor_price < 1%`）。
11. 写日志 `SESSION_QUOTE`。
12. 上游 M16-06 不可用 → `"1005022"`，会话状态保持不变。

#### API-34 手工打标 / 收回标签

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/customers/KH000128/tags
```

```json
{
  "operation": "ADD",
  "tags": [
    {"tag_code": "VIP", "remark": "年度消费超 2 万，店长特批", "effective_to": null},
    {"tag_code": "NIGHT_DRIVER", "remark": "顾客自述经常夜间开车"}
  ]
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "customer_no": "KH000128",
    "added": ["VIP", "NIGHT_DRIVER"],
    "skipped": [],
    "active_tags": ["STUDENT", "HIGH_MYOPIA", "VIP", "NIGHT_DRIVER"]
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. `operation` 支持 `ADD` / `REVOKE`。
2. `ADD`：校验 `tag_code` 在枚举内（`"1005034"`）；已存在生效标签则 `skipped`；新插 `tag_source='MANUAL'`、`confidence=1.000`。
3. `REVOKE`：仅允许收回 `tag_source='MANUAL'` 的标签；自动标签由规则收回（`tag_source='AUTO'` 返回 `"1005007"` 并说明由规则管理）。
4. 收回必须填 `revoke_reason`：置 `is_active=FALSE`、`revoked_at`、`revoke_reason`。
5. 写日志 `TAG_ADD` / `TAG_REVOKE`。
6. 单个顾客手工标签总数 ≤ 20。

3. 走 `idx_m10_customer_tag_code`；结果缓存 10 分钟。

#### API-36 生成关怀任务（人群圈选触达）

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/customers/care-tasks
Idempotency-Key: 2f7e4a1b-8c3d-5e9f-0a4b-6c2d8e1f3a5b
```

```json
{
  "task_name": "高度近视+夜间驾驶 防眩光升级关怀",
  "scene": "PRODUCT_UPGRADE",
  "criteria": {
    "tag_codes": ["HIGH_MYOPIA", "NIGHT_DRIVER"],
    "silent_days_gt": 90,
    "consent_marketing_required": true,
    "customer_status": ["ACTIVE"],
    "store_codes": ["SC001"]
  },
  "channel": "SMS,WECHAT",
  "template_code": "TPL_NIGHT_DRIVING_UPGRADE",
  "scheduled_at": "2026-10-31T18:00:00+08:00",
  "remark": "夜间驾驶镜片新品推广"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "task_no": "GZ202610310005",
    "task_name": "高度近视+夜间驾驶 防眩光升级关怀",
    "matched_count": 236,
    "filtered_by_consent": 42,
    "status": "PENDING",
    "scheduled_at": "2026-10-31T18:00:00+08:00",
    "channel": ["SMS", "WECHAT"],
    "created_at": "2026-10-31T11:30:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验 `criteria` 至少有一个有效条件，否则 `"1005037"`。
2. **合规强制**：`consent_marketing_required` 必须为 `true`（不可关闭），未同意营销触达的顾客一律排除；响应中返回被过滤人数 `filtered_by_consent`。若请求显式传 `false` → `"1005038"`。
3. 圈选命中人数 > 50,000 → `"1005039"`，提示细分条件。
4. 生成任务号 `GZ` + `YYYYMMDD` + 4 位；任务落库（本模块只存任务与人群快照，不存发送明细）。
5. 投递 Celery 任务 `m10_customer_care_dispatch`，分批（200 人/批）调 M03-05 `POST /internal/v1/message/send`。
6. 发送结果回写任务状态（`PENDING` → `RUNNING` → `DONE`/`PARTIAL_FAILED`）。
7. 写日志（`action` 使用 `EXPORT`，`action_group='SECURITY'`，因涉及批量个人信息导出）。

```json
    "amount_distribution": [
      {"bucket": "<1000", "count": 4120},
      {"bucket": "1000-2000", "count": 7860},
      {"bucket": "2000-5000", "count": 5820},
      {"bucket": ">5000", "count": 620}
    ],
    "category_preference": [
      {"item_role": "LENS", "item_role_name": "镜片", "count": 8420, "ratio": 0.4210},
      {"item_role": "FRAME", "item_role_name": "镜架", "count": 6120, "ratio": 0.3060},
      {"item_role": "CONTACT", "item_role_name": "隐形", "count": 2140, "ratio": 0.1070}
    ],
    "repurchase_cycle": {
      "avg_days": 486,
      "median_days": 412,
      "min_days": 92,
      "max_days": 1240,
      "overdue_count": 2680,
      "overdue_high_value_count": 186
    },
    "data_source": {
      "avg_transaction_amount": "M16-04",
      "amount_distribution": "M16-04",
      "funnel": "M10-05",
      "category_preference": "M10-05"
    }
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 日期跨度 ≤ 366 天，否则 `"1005025"`。
2. `kpi.avg_transaction_amount` 与 `amount_distribution` **调 M16-04 统计接口**，本模块不做金额累加；M16 不可用时这两个字段返回 `null` 并置 `degraded=true`。
3. `funnel` 由本模块按 3.4（4）口径统计（会话数据）。
4. `purchase_count_distribution` / `repurchase_cycle` 由 `m10_customer` 快照聚合。
5. `category_preference` 按 `m10_selection_draft` 中 `draft_status='MOVED_TO_ORDER'` 的行统计。
6. 结果缓存 Redis `m10:customer:analysis:{store}:{from}:{to}` TTL 600s。

**业务逻辑：**

3. `category_preference` / `brand_preference` 从 `m10_selection_draft` 的已转单行聚合。
4. `purchase_history` 调 M10-06 与 M16-04 只读获取（`amount` 来自 M16-04）。
5. `suggested_action` 按复购周期与标签生成建议文案。

#### API-39 取顾客档案（内部契约 P-10）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m10/customers/KH000128
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "customer_no": "KH000128",
    "customer_name": "王女士",
    "gender": "F",
    "age": 32,
    "phone": "13800001234",
    "phone_mask": "138****1234",
    "patient_id": null,
    "empi_no": null,
    "link_status": "UNLINKED",
    "customer_type": "ADULT",
    "customer_status": "ACTIVE",
    "source": "WALK_IN",
    "member_flag": true,
    "member_no": "VM00001234",
    "member_level": "GOLD",
    "allergy_history": "金属镜架过敏",
    "has_purchased": true,
    "purchase_count": 3,
    "total_amount": 6812.00,
    "last_purchase_at": "2026-08-12T10:05:00+08:00",
    "tag_codes": ["HIGH_MYOPIA", "DR_RETINA"],
    "store_code": "SC001",
    "query_time": "2026-10-31T10:12:03+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 按 `customer_no` 查主表；不存在返回 `"1005001"`（HTTP 404）。
2. 不返回身份证密文；手机号明文仅在内部契约白名单调用方（M16-04/M20）下返回，其余调用方返回掩码。
3. `patient_id` 可能为 `null`——**这是合法返回值**，调用方必须能处理（M16-04 开单时 `patient_id` 可为空）。
4. 结果缓存 Redis `m10:customer:brief:{customer_no}` TTL 60s；顾客变更时主动失效。
5. SLA：P95 ≤ 100ms（与共享规范 8 章一致）。

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "customer_no": "KH000341",
    "customer_name": "张先生",
    "phone_mask": "137****0001",
    "patient_id": null,
    "created": false,
    "matched_by": "PENDING_REVIEW",
    "match_confidence": 0.62,
    "pending_review": true,
    "review_reason": "手机号命中 2 个同名候选顾客，无法自动判定归属",
    "candidates": [
      {
        "customer_no": "KH000341",
        "customer_name": "张先生",
        "gender": "M",
        "age": 35,
        "phone_mask": "137****0001",
        "patient_id": null,
        "link_status": "UNLINKED",
        "customer_type": "ADULT",
        "customer_status": "ACTIVE",
        "source": "WALK_IN",
        "member_flag": false,
        "member_level": null,
        "purchase_count": 1,

        "tags": [],
        "last_visit_at": "2025-05-20T14:00:00+08:00"
      },

        "customer_no": "KH000878",
        "customer_name": "张先生",
        "gender": "M",
        "age": 36,
        "phone_mask": "137****0001",
        "patient_id": null,
        "link_status": "UNLINKED",
        "customer_type": "ADULT",

      }
    ],
    "idempotent": false
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑 —— 幂等匹配算法（本接口的核心）：**

```
输入：customer_name, phone, patient_id(可选), caller

步骤 0：参数校验
  - phone 必须符合手机号/座机格式，否则 "1005003"（无法建档案时必须失败，不能静默跳过）
  - customer_name 不得为空白，否则 40001

步骤 1：幂等键检查
  若存在 Idempotency-Key 且 Redis m10:idem:customer:ensure:{key} 有值
    → 直接返回缓存结果（idempotent = true）

步骤 2：手机号精确匹配（第一优先级）
  phone_hash = sha256(phone + salt)
  查 m10_customer WHERE phone_hash = :h AND is_deleted = FALSE
  分三种情况：

  ┌─ 情况 A：命中 0 条
  │    → 进入步骤 3（尝试按 patient_id 匹配）
  │
  ├─ 情况 B：命中 1 条，且 customer_status = 'ACTIVE'
  │    ├─ B1：姓名完全一致（去空格后相等）
  │    │     → matched_by = "PHONE_NAME", match_confidence = 1.0
  │    │     → created = false，直接返回（这是最常见的幂等路径）
  │    └─ B2：姓名不一致
  │          → matched_by = "PHONE_ONLY", match_confidence = 0.75
  │          → pending_review = true
  │          → review_reason = "手机号一致但姓名不一致（原：王女士 / 传入：王小雨），
  │             可能为家属共用手机号，已进入人工确认队列"
  │          → 返回该顾客（created = false），同时写 PENDING 关联日志
  │
  ├─ 情况 C：命中 1 条，但 customer_status = 'MERGED'
  │    → 沿 merged_into_no 递归解析到最终目标顾客（最多 3 层防环）
  │    → 返回目标顾客，matched_by = "PHONE_NAME"，created = false
  │    → idempotent = true
  │
  └─ 情况 D：命中 ≥ 2 条（理论上被唯一索引阻止，仅可能发生在历史脏数据或
              部分 MERGED 记录之外）
       → 按候选排序打分，返回得分最高者
       → matched_by = "PENDING_REVIEW", pending_review = true
       → candidates 返回全部候选（最多 5 条）
       → 写 m10_customer_log（action=LINK_PATIENT, result=REJECTED,
         error_code="1005040"）供人工核查

步骤 3：按 patient_id 匹配（第二优先级，仅在传入 patient_id 时）
  查 m10_customer WHERE patient_id = :pid AND is_deleted = FALSE
  ├─ 命中 1 条 → matched_by = "PHONE_NAME"（沿用既有顾客），
  │              并把该顾客手机号与传入手机号比对：
  │              不一致时仅更新备用电话，不覆盖主手机号
  └─ 命中 0 条 → 进入步骤 4

步骤 4：新建顾客
  - 生成 customer_no（Redis INCR m10:code:KH）
  - data_source = 'ENSURE'（标记由内部契约建档）
  - source 取请求值，来源关联 source_ref（如 M16 销售单号）
  - 若传入 patient_id → link_status = 'LINKED'，link_source = 'ENSURE'
  - 若未传 patient_id → link_status = 'UNLINKED'（这是本接口最重要的语义：
    即使 M16-04 不知道患者，也能建出合法顾客档案）
  - 自动打标：NEW_CUSTOMER（建档即打）
  - 写日志 CREATE（operator_role = 'SYSTEM'）
  - 发布事件 m10.customer.created
  - matched_by = "CREATED", match_confidence = 1.0, created = true

步骤 5：写幂等键
  Redis m10:idem:customer:ensure:{key} = 结果 JSON，TTL 24h

并发保护：
  - 手机号唯一索引 uk_m10_customer_phone 兜底，两个请求同时新建时
    第二个抛唯一约束异常 → 捕获异常后重新按手机号查询并返回既有顾客
    （"插入冲突即重查"模式），保证幂等
```

**关键约定：**

| 约定 | 说明 |
|---|---|
| **幂等性** | 同一手机号 + 同一姓名重复调用，永远返回同一 `customer_no`，`created = false` |
| **不覆盖** | 已存在顾客的手机号、姓名、身份证**不被覆盖**；只在姓名不一致时进人工确认队列 |
| **`patient_id` 可空** | M16-04 不传 `patient_id` 是合法调用，返回 `patient_id = null` |
| **不写 M03** | 本接口**绝不**调用 M03-01 ensure 建患者；只建顾客 |
| **失败语义** | 只有「手机号格式非法」或「系统异常」才返回错误；任何可建档案的情形都必须成功返回 |
| **SLA** | P95 ≤ 200ms（与共享规范 8 章一致） |

### 6.4 FastAPI 路由实现示例

```python
# app/api/v1/m10/customer.py
"""M10-05 顾客与选配工作台 —— 路由层"""

from typing import List, Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Body, Depends, Path, Query, Request
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.database import get_db
from app.core.response import PageResult, success_response
from app.core.security import get_current_user, require_permission
from app.schemas.m10.customer import (
    AddressIn, ConsumptionAnalysisOut, CustomerCreateIn, CustomerDetailOut,
    CustomerEnsureIn, CustomerLinkPatientIn, CustomerMergeIn, CustomerPhoneChangeIn,
    CustomerQuery, CustomerTagAddIn, CustomerTagRevokeIn, CustomerUpdateIn,
    QuoteOut, ReplacementOut, SelectionAbandonIn, SelectionDraftAddIn,
    SelectionDraftUpdateIn, SelectionLookupIn, SelectionQuoteIn,
    SelectionReplaceIn, SelectionSessionCreateIn, SelectionSessionHandoverIn,
    SelectionSessionOut, WorkbenchQuery,
)
from app.services.m10.customer_service import CustomerService
from app.services.m10.customer_selection_service import CustomerSelectionService
from app.services.m10.customer_quote_service import CustomerQuoteService

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/m10/customers", tags=["M10-05 顾客与选配"])
internal_router = APIRouter(prefix="/internal/v1/m10/customers",
                            tags=["M10-05 内部契约"])


# ==================== 前台工作台 ====================
@router.get("/workbench/overview", summary="工作台今日概览指标")
async def workbench_overview(
    store_code: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=32),
    work_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="业务日期 YYYY-MM-DD"),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:customer:view")),
):
    """返回到店/选配/成交/实收/待取/待收六项指标。"""
    svc = CustomerSelectionService(db)
    data = await svc.workbench_overview(store_code, work_date, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/workbench/pickup-todo", summary="今日取镜待办")
async def workbench_pickup_todo(
    store_code: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=32),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:customer:view")),
):
    """聚合 M10-06 取镜待办，本模块只读展示。"""
    svc = CustomerSelectionService(db)
    items, total = await svc.pickup_todo(store_code, page, page_size, user)
    return success_response(PageResult(
        items=items, total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size,
        pages=(total + page_size - 1) // page_size))


@router.get("/workbench/pending", summary="待验光/待加工/待收款汇总")
async def workbench_pending(
    store_code: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=32),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:customer:view")),
):
    """三块上游数据并行拉取，任一不可用时该块降级为空。"""
    svc = CustomerSelectionService(db)
    data = await svc.workbench_pending(store_code, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/workbench/appointments", summary="今日预约清单")
async def workbench_appointments(
    store_code: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=32),
    work_date: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:customer:view")),
):
    """聚合验光预约、复查预约、取镜预约三类。"""
    svc = CustomerSelectionService(db)
    data = await svc.workbench_appointments(store_code, work_date, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/workbench/my-sessions", summary="我的接待中会话")
async def workbench_my_sessions(
    salesperson_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:customer:view")),
):
    """返回当前销售员进行中的选配会话。"""
    svc = CustomerSelectionService(db)
    data = await svc.my_sessions(salesperson_id, user)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== 顾客档案 ====================
@router.get("", summary="顾客分页列表")
async def list_customers(
    keyword: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=64),
    phone_suffix: Optional[str] = Query(None, min_length=4, max_length=4),
    source: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    customer_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    customer_status: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    link_status: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    tag_codes: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    churn_risk: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    last_visit_from: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    last_visit_to: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    store_code: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    order_by: str = Query("last_visit_at"),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:customer:view")),
):
    """多条件检索顾客，支持手机尾号与标签交集匹配。"""
    q = CustomerQuery(
        keyword=keyword, phone_suffix=phone_suffix,
        source=source.split(",") if source else None,
        customer_type=customer_type.split(",") if customer_type else None,
        customer_status=customer_status.split(",") if customer_status else None,
        link_status=link_status.split(",") if link_status else None,
        tag_codes=tag_codes.split(",") if tag_codes else None,
        churn_risk=churn_risk.split(",") if churn_risk else None,
        last_visit_from=last_visit_from, last_visit_to=last_visit_to,
        store_code=store_code, order_by=order_by,
        page=page, page_size=page_size,
    )
    svc = CustomerService(db)
    items, total = await svc.list_customers(q, user)
    return success_response(PageResult(
        items=items, total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size,
        pages=(total + page_size - 1) // page_size))


@router.get("/check-phone", summary="手机号查重")
async def check_phone(
    phone: str = Query(..., min_length=7, max_length=20),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:customer:view")),
):
    """建档前查重，同时返回 M03 候选患者供前台确认关联。"""
    svc = CustomerService(db)
    data = await svc.check_phone(phone, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/tags/statistics", summary="标签分布统计")
async def tag_statistics(
    store_code: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=32),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:customer:tag")),
):
    """按分组返回标签人数与占比。"""
    svc = CustomerService(db)
    data = await svc.tag_statistics(store_code, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/analysis/statistics", summary="消费分析总看板")
async def analysis_statistics(
    date_from: str = Query(...),
    date_to: str = Query(...),
    store_code: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=32),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:customer:stat")),
):
    """KPI、漏斗、分布、复购周期；金额类指标调 M16-04。"""
    svc = CustomerService(db)

```

### 6.5 Service 层核心逻辑示例

```python
# app/services/m10/customer_service.py
"""M10-05 顾客服务：建档、查重、幂等 ensure、单向关联、合并。"""

import hashlib
import json
from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from sqlalchemy import and_, func, or_, select, text
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.clients.m10.m03_client import M03Client
from app.core.exceptions import BizException
from app.core.redis import redis_client
from app.models.m10.customer import M10Customer, M10CustomerExt
from app.models.m10.customer_log import M10CustomerLog
from app.models.m10.customer_tag import M10CustomerTag
from app.schemas.m10.customer import (
    CustomerCreateIn, CustomerEnsureIn, CustomerLinkPatientIn,
    CustomerMergeIn, CustomerPhoneChangeIn, CustomerQuery, CustomerUpdateIn,
)

CODE_KEY = "m10:code:KH"
IDEM_PREFIX = "m10:idem:customer:ensure:"
PHONE_SALT_KEY = "m10:cust:phone_salt"


def mask_phone(phone: Optional[str]) -> Optional[str]:
    """手机号掩码：138****1234。"""
    if not phone or len(phone) < 7:
        return phone
    return phone[:3] + "****" + phone[-4:]


def phone_hash(phone: str, salt: str) -> str:
    """计算手机号哈希，用于唯一索引与幂等匹配。"""
    return hashlib.sha256((phone + salt).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


class CustomerService:
    """顾客档案服务。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        """初始化。"""
        self.db = db
        self.m03 = M03Client()

    # ---------- 内部工具 ----------
    async def _salt(self) -> str:
        """取手机号哈希盐（KMS 托管，缓存于 Redis）。"""
        salt = await redis_client.get(PHONE_SALT_KEY)
        if salt is None:
            raise BizException("50000", "手机号哈希盐未配置")
        return salt

    async def _next_customer_no(self) -> str:
        """生成顾客号 KH + 6 位流水。"""
        seq = await redis_client.incr(CODE_KEY)
        if seq > 999999:
            raise BizException("50000", "顾客号段已耗尽，请联系信息科扩容")
        return "KH%06d" % seq

    async def _get_by_no(self, customer_no: str) -> M10Customer:
        """按顾客号取主表，不存在抛错。"""
        row = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M10Customer).where(
                M10Customer.customer_no == customer_no,
                M10Customer.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if row is None:
            raise BizException("1005001", "顾客不存在或已停用")
        return row

    async def _write_log(self, action: str, action_group: str,
                         customer: Optional[M10Customer],
                         changed_fields: Optional[List[str]] = None,
                         before: Optional[dict] = None,
                         after: Optional[dict] = None,
                         summary: Optional[str] = None,
                         reason: Optional[str] = None,
                         is_sensitive: bool = False,
                         approval_no: Optional[str] = None,
                         operator: Any = None,
                         ip: Optional[str] = None,
                         result: str = "SUCCESS",
                         error_code: Optional[str] = None,
                         error_msg: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
        """写审计日志（只追加）。"""
        log_no = "CL%s%08d" % (datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d"),
                               await redis_client.incr("m10:code:CL"))
        self.db.add(M10CustomerLog(
            log_no=log_no,
            customer_id=customer.id if customer else None,
            customer_no=customer.customer_no if customer else None,
            customer_name_mask=(customer.customer_name[:1] + "**"
                                if customer else None),
            action=action,
            action_group=action_group,
            changed_fields=",".join(changed_fields) if changed_fields else None,
            before_snapshot=before,
            after_snapshot=after,
            change_summary=summary,
            change_reason=reason,
            is_sensitive=is_sensitive,
            approval_no=approval_no,
            operator_id=getattr(operator, "id", None),
            operator_name=getattr(operator, "name", None),
            operator_role=getattr(operator, "role", None),
            operator_ip=ip,
            result=result,
            error_code=error_code,
            error_msg=error_msg,
        ))

    # ---------- 建档 ----------
    async def create(self, payload: CustomerCreateIn, operator: Any,
                     ip: Optional[str], idem_key: Optional[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """新建顾客：手机号唯一，生成顾客号，事务写 4 张表。"""
        salt = await self._salt()
        p_hash = phone_hash(payload.phone, salt)

        # 1. 手机号查重
        exists = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M10Customer).where(
                M10Customer.phone_hash == p_hash,
                M10Customer.is_deleted.is_(False),
                M10Customer.customer_status != "MERGED")
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if exists is not None:
            raise BizException(
                "1005002",
                "手机号已存在顾客档案（%s），请直接检索该顾客" % exists.customer_no,
                data={"existing_customer_no": exists.customer_no,
                      "existing_customer_name": exists.customer_name})

        # 2. 身份证查重
        id_hash = None
        if payload.id_card_no:
            id_hash = hashlib.sha256(
                payload.id_card_no.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
            dup = (await self.db.execute(
                select(M10Customer).where(
                    M10Customer.id_card_hash == id_hash,
                    M10Customer.is_deleted.is_(False))
            )).scalar_one_or_none()
            if dup is not None:
                raise BizException(
                    "1005005",
                    "身份证号已存在顾客档案（%s）" % dup.customer_no)

        # 3. 生成顾客号并组装主表
        customer_no = await self._next_customer_no()
        age, age_group = self._calc_age(payload.birth_date)
        customer = M10Customer(
            customer_no=customer_no,
            patient_id=None,
            link_status="UNLINKED",
            customer_name=payload.customer_name,
            name_pinyin=None,
            gender=payload.gender,
            birth_date=payload.birth_date,
            age=age,
            age_group=age_group,
            phone=payload.phone,
            phone_hash=p_hash,
            phone2=payload.phone2,
            id_card_hash=id_hash,
            id_card_mask=(payload.id_card_no[:3] + "***********"
                          + payload.id_card_no[-4:]
                          if payload.id_card_no else None),
            id_card_type=payload.id_card_type,
            source=payload.source,
            source_ref=payload.source_ref,
            referrer_customer_no=payload.referrer_customer_no,
            customer_type=payload.customer_type,
            customer_status="ACTIVE",
            allergy_history=payload.allergy_history,
            medical_history=payload.medical_history,
            glasses_history=payload.glasses_history,
            consent_marketing=payload.consent_marketing,
            consent_at=datetime.now() if payload.consent_marketing else None,
            data_source="MANUAL",
            store_code=payload.store_code,
            remark=payload.remark,
            created_by=getattr(operator, "id", None),
        )
        self.db.add(customer)
        try:
            await self.db.flush()
        except IntegrityError:
            await self.db.rollback()
            # 并发插入冲突：重查并返回既有顾客，保证幂等
            dup = (await self.db.execute(
                select(M10Customer).where(
                    M10Customer.phone_hash == p_hash,
                    M10Customer.is_deleted.is_(False))
            )).scalar_one_or_none()
            if dup is not None:
                raise BizException("1005002", "手机号已存在顾客档案",
                                   data={"existing_customer_no": dup.customer_no})
            raise BizException("50000", "顾客建档失败，请重试")

        # 4. 扩展表（画像）
        if payload.ext is not None:
            self.db.add(self._build_ext(customer, payload.ext))

        # 5. 手工标签
        for tag_code in payload.tag_codes:
            self.db.add(M10CustomerTag(
                customer_id=customer.id,
                customer_no=customer.customer_no,
                tag_code=tag_code,
                tag_name=tag_code,
                tag_group="BUSINESS",
                tag_source="MANUAL",
                confidence=Decimal("1.000"),
                effective_from=date.today(),
                created_by=getattr(operator, "id", None),
            ))

        # 6. 日志与提交
        await self._write_log("CREATE", "CUSTOMER", customer,
                              summary="新建顾客档案，来源 %s" % payload.source,
                              operator=operator, ip=ip)
        await self.db.commit()

        # 7. 异步匹配 M03 候选患者（不自动回填 patient_id）
        candidates = await self._match_patient_candidates(payload.phone)
        return {
            "customer_no": customer.customer_no,
            "customer_name": customer.customer_name,
            "link_status": customer.link_status,
            "patient_id": customer.patient_id,
            "tags": payload.tag_codes,
            "candidate_patients": candidates,
            "created_at": customer.created_at,
        }

    def _calc_age(self, birth_date: Optional[date]) -> Tuple[Optional[int], Optional[str]]:
        """按出生日期计算年龄与年龄段。"""
        if birth_date is None:
            return None, None
        today = date.today()
        age = today.year - birth_date.year - (
            (today.month, today.day) < (birth_date.month, birth_date.day))
        if age <= 6:
            group = "CHILD"
        elif age <= 17:
            group = "JUNIOR"
        elif age <= 39:
            group = "ADULT"
        elif age <= 59:
            group = "MIDDLE"
        else:
            group = "SENIOR"
        return age, group

    # ---------- P-11 幂等 ensure ----------
    async def ensure(self, payload: CustomerEnsureIn,
                     idem_key: Optional[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """确保顾客存在：手机号 + 姓名匹配，命中返回既有，未命中新建。

        这是 M16-04 开单前调用的内部契约（P-11），必须保证：
        1) 幂等：同参数重复调用返回同一 customer_no；
        2) 不覆盖：已存在顾客的信息不被请求体覆盖；
        3) patient_id 可空：M16 不传患者 ID 也能建出合法档案。
        """
        cache_key = IDEM_PREFIX + (idem_key or "")
        if idem_key:
            cached = await redis_client.get(cache_key)
            if cached:
                data = json.loads(cached)
                data["idempotent"] = True
                return data

        salt = await self._salt()
        p_hash = phone_hash(payload.phone, salt)
        review_reason = None
        candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        # 步骤 2：手机号精确匹配
        rows = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M10Customer).where(
                M10Customer.phone_hash == p_hash,
                M10Customer.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalars().all()
        active = [r for r in rows if r.customer_status == "ACTIVE"]

        if len(active) == 1:
            target = active[0]
            name_equal = target.customer_name.strip() == payload.customer_name.strip()
            if name_equal:
                result = self._ensure_out(target, "PHONE_NAME", Decimal("1.0"),
                                          False, None, [])
                await self._cache_idem(cache_key, result, idem_key)
                return result
            # B2：手机号一致但姓名不一致 → 进人工确认队列
            review_reason = (
                "手机号一致但姓名不一致（原：%s / 传入：%s），"
                "可能为家属共用手机号，已进入人工确认队列"
                % (target.customer_name, payload.customer_name))
            await self._write_log(
                "LINK_PATIENT", "LINK", target,
                summary=review_reason,
                is_sensitive=True,
                reason=payload.caller,
                result="REJECTED",
                error_code="1005040",
                error_msg=review_reason)
            result = self._ensure_out(target, "PHONE_ONLY", Decimal("0.75"),
                                      True, review_reason, [])
            await self.db.commit()
            await self._cache_idem(cache_key, result, idem_key)
            return result

        if len(active) > 1:
            # D：多候选，返回得分最高者并进入人工确认
            candidates = [self._brief(r) for r in active[:5]]
            target = active[0]
            review_reason = ("手机号命中 %d 个活跃顾客，无法自动判定归属，"
                             "已进入人工确认队列" % len(active))
            await self._write_log(
                "LINK_PATIENT", "LINK", target, summary=review_reason,
                is_sensitive=True, reason=payload.caller,
                result="REJECTED", error_code="1005040", error_msg=review_reason)
            result = self._ensure_out(target, "PENDING_REVIEW", Decimal("0.62"),
                                      True, review_reason, candidates)
            await self.db.commit()
            return result

        # 情况 C：命中但均已合并 → 沿 merged_into_no 解析
        if rows:
            target = await self._resolve_merged(rows[0])
            result = self._ensure_out(target, "PHONE_NAME", Decimal("1.0"),
                                      False, None, [])
            await self._cache_idem(cache_key, result, idem_key)
            return result

        # 步骤 3：按 patient_id 匹配
        if payload.patient_id:
            by_pid = (await self.db.execute(
                select(M10Customer).where(
                    M10Customer.patient_id == payload.patient_id,
                    M10Customer.is_deleted.is_(False))
            )).scalar_one_or_none()
            if by_pid is not None:
                result = self._ensure_out(by_pid, "PHONE_NAME", Decimal("1.0"),
                                          False, None, [])
                await self._cache_idem(cache_key, result, idem_key)
                return result

        # 步骤 4：新建
        customer_no = await self._next_customer_no()
        customer = M10Customer(
            customer_no=customer_no,
            patient_id=payload.patient_id,
            link_status="LINKED" if payload.patient_id else "UNLINKED",
            linked_at=datetime.now() if payload.patient_id else None,
            link_source="ENSURE" if payload.patient_id else None,
            customer_name=payload.customer_name,
            gender=payload.gender or "U",
            birth_date=payload.birth_date,
            phone=payload.phone,
            phone_hash=p_hash,
            source=payload.source,
            source_ref=payload.source_ref,
            customer_type="ADULT",
            customer_status="ACTIVE",
            data_source="ENSURE",
            store_code=payload.store_code,
        )
        self.db.add(customer)
        try:
            await self.db.flush()
        except IntegrityError:
            await self.db.rollback()
            dup = (await self.db.execute(
                select(M10Customer).where(M10Customer.phone_hash == p_hash,
                                          M10Customer.is_deleted.is_(False))
            )).scalar_one()
            result = self._ensure_out(dup, "PHONE_NAME", Decimal("1.0"),
                                      False, None, [])
            await self._cache_idem(cache_key, result, idem_key)
            return result

        self.db.add(M10CustomerTag(
            customer_id=customer.id, customer_no=customer.customer_no,
            tag_code="NEW_CUSTOMER", tag_name="新客户", tag_group="MARKETING",
            tag_source="AUTO", rule_code="RULE_NEW_CUSTOMER",
            confidence=Decimal("1.000"), effective_from=date.today()))
        await self._write_log("CREATE", "CUSTOMER", customer,
                              summary="由 %s 通过 P-11 ensure 建档" % payload.caller,
                              operator=None)
        await self.db.commit()

        result = self._ensure_out(customer, "CREATED", Decimal("1.0"),
                                  False, None, [])
        await self._cache_idem(cache_key, result, idem_key)
        return result

    def _ensure_out(self, c: M10Customer, matched_by: str,
                    confidence: Decimal, pending: bool,
                    reason: Optional[str],
                    candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """组装 ensure 响应。"""
        return {
            "customer_no": c.customer_no,
            "customer_name": c.customer_name,
            "phone_mask": mask_phone(c.phone),
            "patient_id": c.patient_id,
            "created": matched_by == "CREATED",
            "matched_by": matched_by,
            "match_confidence": float(confidence),
            "pending_review": pending,
            "review_reason": reason,
            "candidates": candidates,
            "idempotent": matched_by != "CREATED",
        }

    async def _cache_idem(self, cache_key: str, result: Dict[str, Any],
                          idem_key: Optional[str]) -> None:
        """缓存幂等结果 24 小时。"""
        if idem_key and cache_key.endswith(idem_key):
            await redis_client.setex(cache_key, 86400, json.dumps(result,
                                                                 default=str))

    async def _resolve_merged(self, customer: M10Customer,
                              depth: int = 0) -> M10Customer:
        """沿 merged_into_no 解析到最终目标（最多 3 层防环）。"""
        if customer.customer_status != "MERGED" or depth >= 3:
            return customer
        target = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M10Customer).where(
                M10Customer.customer_no == customer.merged_into_no,
                M10Customer.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if target is None:
            return customer
        return await self._resolve_merged(target, depth + 1)

    def _brief(self, c: M10Customer) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """顾客摘要。"""
        return {
            "customer_no": c.customer_no,
            "customer_name": c.customer_name,
            "gender": c.gender,
            "age": c.age,
            "phone_mask": mask_phone(c.phone),
            "patient_id": c.patient_id,
            "link_status": c.link_status,
            "customer_type": c.customer_type,
            "customer_status": c.customer_status,
            "source": c.source,
            "member_flag": c.member_flag,
            "member_level": c.member_level,
            "purchase_count": c.purchase_count,
            "last_purchase_at": c.last_purchase_at,
            "tags": [],
            "last_visit_at": c.last_visit_at,
        }

    def _build_ext(self, customer: M10Customer, ext: Any) -> M10CustomerExt:
        """构建扩展表记录。"""
        return M10CustomerExt(
            customer_id=customer.id,
            customer_no=customer.customer_no,
            occupation=ext.occupation,
            occupation_class=ext.occupation_class,
            usage_scene=",".join(ext.usage_scene) if ext.usage_scene else None,
            primary_scene=ext.primary_scene,
            daily_screen_hours=ext.daily_screen_hours,
            outdoor_hours=ext.outdoor_hours,
            driving_need=ext.driving_need,
            driving_license=ext.driving_license,
            night_driving_freq=ext.night_driving_freq,
            sports_need=ext.sports_need,
            face_shape=ext.face_shape,
            face_width_level=ext.face_width_level,
            skin_sensitive=ext.skin_sensitive,
            weight_sensitive=ext.weight_sensitive,
            previous_concerns=ext.previous_concerns,
            preferred_brand=(",".join(ext.preferred_brand)
                             if ext.preferred_brand else None),
            lens_pref_coating=(",".join(ext.lens_pref_coating)
                               if ext.lens_pref_coating else None),
            myopia_control_need=ext.myopia_control_need,
        )

    async def _match_patient_candidates(self, phone: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """调 M03-01 按手机号匹配患者候选（降级返回空）。"""
        try:
            return await self.m03.match_by_phone(phone)
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
            return []

    # ---------- 单向关联 patient_id ----------
    async def link_patient(self, customer_no: str, payload: CustomerLinkPatientIn,
                           operator: Any, ip: Optional[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """回填 patient_id：只写 m10_customer，绝不写 M03。"""
        customer = await self._get_by_no(customer_no)
        if customer.customer_status == "MERGED":
            raise BizException("1005019", "顾客已合并，请使用目标顾客号 %s"
                               % customer.merged_into_no)
        if customer.link_status == "LINKED":
            raise BizException("1005007", "该顾客已关联患者档案，不可重复回填")

        # 患者是否已被其他顾客关联
        other = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M10Customer).where(
                M10Customer.patient_id == payload.patient_id,
                M10Customer.id != customer.id,
                M10Customer.is_deleted.is_(False),
                M10Customer.customer_status == "ACTIVE")
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if other is not None:
            raise BizException(
                "1005040",
                "患者 %d 已关联顾客 %s，存在关联冲突"
                % (payload.patient_id, other.customer_no),
                data={"conflict_customer_no": other.customer_no})

        # 手机号一致性校验
        verified = False
        if payload.verify_phone:
            brief = await self.m03.get_patient_brief(payload.patient_id)
            if brief is None:
                raise BizException("1005040", "未取到患者档案，无法校验手机号")
            if brief.get("phone") != customer.phone:
                raise BizException(
                    "1005040",
                    "顾客手机号与患者档案手机号不一致，请人工核实后再关联")
            verified = True

        before = {"patient_id": None, "link_status": customer.link_status}
        customer.patient_id = payload.patient_id
        customer.link_status = "LINKED"
        customer.linked_at = datetime.now()
        customer.linked_by = getattr(operator, "id", None)
        customer.link_source = payload.link_source
        await self._write_log(
            "LINK_PATIENT", "LINK", customer,
            changed_fields=["patient_id", "link_status"],
            before=before,
            after={"patient_id": payload.patient_id, "link_status": "LINKED"},
            summary="回填患者 ID=%d，来源 %s" % (payload.patient_id,
                                                payload.link_source),
            reason=payload.reason,
            is_sensitive=True,
            approval_no=None,
            operator=operator, ip=ip)
        await self.db.commit()
        return {
            "customer_no": customer.customer_no,
            "patient_id": customer.patient_id,
            "link_status": customer.link_status,
            "link_source": customer.link_source,
            "phone_verified": verified,
            "linked_at": customer.linked_at,
        }

    # ---------- 合并 ----------
    async def merge(self, payload: CustomerMergeIn, operator: Any,
                    ip: Optional[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """顾客档案合并：来源置 MERGED，会话/标签/地址重定向到目标。"""
        if payload.target_customer_no in payload.source_customer_nos:
            raise BizException("1005029", "合并目标不能同时作为合并来源")
        if len(payload.source_customer_nos) > 10:
            raise BizException("1005031", "单次合并来源不超过 10 个")

        target = await self._get_by_no(payload.target_customer_no)
        if target.customer_status != "ACTIVE":
            raise BizException("1005030", "合并目标顾客不存在或状态异常")

        merged = []
        for src_no in payload.source_customer_nos:
            src = await self._get_by_no(src_no)
            if src.customer_status != "ACTIVE":
                raise BizException("1005030", "来源顾客 %s 状态异常" % src_no)
            # 不同患者不可合并
            if (src.patient_id and target.patient_id
                    and src.patient_id != target.patient_id):
                raise BizException(
                    "1005040",
                    "来源顾客 %s 与目标顾客关联了不同患者，疑似不同的人" % src_no)

            # 会话重定向
            moved_sessions = (await self.db.execute(
                text("UPDATE m10_selection_session SET customer_id = :tid, "
                     "customer_no = :tno WHERE customer_id = :sid "
                     "AND is_deleted = FALSE RETURNING id"),
                {"tid": target.id, "tno": target.customer_no, "sid": src.id}
            )).rowcount
            # 标签迁移
            moved_tags = (await self.db.execute(
                text("UPDATE m10_customer_tag t SET is_active = FALSE, "
                     "revoked_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, "
                     "revoke_reason = '档案合并' "
                     "WHERE t.customer_id = :sid AND t.is_active = TRUE "
                     "AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM m10_customer_tag d "
                     "WHERE d.customer_id = :tid AND d.tag_code = t.tag_code "
                     "AND d.is_active = TRUE)"),
                {"sid": src.id, "tid": target.id}
            )).rowcount
            await self.db.execute(
                text("UPDATE m10_customer_tag SET customer_id = :tid, "
                     "customer_no = :tno WHERE customer_id = :sid"),
                {"tid": target.id, "tno": target.customer_no, "sid": src.id})
            # 地址迁移
            await self.db.execute(
                text("UPDATE m10_customer_address SET customer_id = :tid, "
                     "customer_no = :tno, is_default = FALSE "
                     "WHERE customer_id = :sid"),
                {"tid": target.id, "tno": target.customer_no, "sid": src.id})

            src.customer_status = "MERGED"
            src.merged_into_no = target.customer_no
            await self._write_log(
                "MERGE_SOURCE", "MERGE", src,
                changed_fields=["customer_status", "merged_into_no"],
                summary="档案合并至 %s" % target.customer_no,
                reason=payload.merge_reason, is_sensitive=True,
                approval_no=payload.approval_no, operator=operator, ip=ip)
            merged.append({"customer_no": src_no,
                           "moved_sessions": moved_sessions,
                           "moved_tags": moved_tags})

        target.version += 1
        await self._write_log(
            "MERGE_TARGET", "MERGE", target,
            summary="接收合并：%s" % ",".join(payload.source_customer_nos),
            reason=payload.merge_reason, is_sensitive=True,
            approval_no=payload.approval_no, operator=operator, ip=ip)
        await self.db.commit()
        return {
            "target_customer_no": target.customer_no,
            "merged_sources": merged,
            "target_version": target.version,
            "merged_at": datetime.now(),
        }

    # ---------- 手机号变更（敏感） ----------
    async def change_phone(self, customer_no: str,
                           payload: CustomerPhoneChangeIn,
                           operator: Any, ip: Optional[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """变更手机号：必须填原因与审批单号，写敏感日志。"""
        if not payload.change_reason or len(payload.change_reason.strip()) < 4:
            raise BizException("1005011", "变更手机号必须填写原因与审批单号")
        if not payload.approval_no:
            raise BizException("1005011", "变更手机号必须填写原因与审批单号")

        customer = await self._get_by_no(customer_no)
        salt = await self._salt()
        new_hash = phone_hash(payload.new_phone, salt)
        if new_hash == customer.phone_hash:
            raise BizException("40001", "新手机号与原手机号相同")

        dup = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M10Customer).where(
                M10Customer.phone_hash == new_hash,
                M10Customer.is_deleted.is_(False),
                M10Customer.customer_status != "MERGED")
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if dup is not None:
            raise BizException("1005002", "新手机号已被顾客 %s 使用"
                               % dup.customer_no)

        before = {"phone": mask_phone(customer.phone)}
        customer.phone = payload.new_phone
        customer.phone_hash = new_hash
        await self._write_log(
            "PHONE_CHANGE", "CUSTOMER", customer,
            changed_fields=["phone"],
            before=before,
            after={"phone": mask_phone(payload.new_phone)},
            summary="手机号由 %s 变更为 %s"
                    % (mask_phone(before["phone"]),
                       mask_phone(payload.new_phone)),
            reason=payload.change_reason,
            is_sensitive=True,
            approval_no=payload.approval_no,
            operator=operator, ip=ip)
        await self.db.commit()
        return {
            "customer_no": customer.customer_no,
            "phone_mask": mask_phone(customer.phone),
            "changed_at": datetime.now(),
        }
```

```python
# app/services/m10/customer_selection_service.py
"""M10-05 选配会话服务：会话生命周期、草稿、光度校验、替换推荐。"""

import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from sqlalchemy import and_, func, select, text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.clients.m10.m01_optical_client import M01OpticalClient
from app.clients.m10.m10_rx_client import M10PrescriptionClient
from app.core.exceptions import BizException
from app.core.redis import redis_client
from app.models.m10.customer import M10Customer
from app.models.m10.selection import M10SelectionDraft, M10SelectionSession
from app.schemas.m10.customer import (
    SelectionAbandonIn, SelectionDraftAddIn, SelectionDraftUpdateIn,
    SelectionReplaceIn, SelectionSessionCreateIn, SelectionSessionHandoverIn,
)

SESSION_KEY = "m10:selection:%s"
SESSION_TTL = 4 * 3600
MAX_CONCURRENT_SESSIONS = 3
MAX_EXTEND_MINUTES = 240


class CustomerSelectionService:
    """选配会话与选品服务。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        """初始化。"""
        self.db = db
        self.optical = M01OpticalClient()
        self.rx_client = M10PrescriptionClient()

    async def _load_session(self, session_no: str) -> M10SelectionSession:
        """取会话，不存在返回 1005013。"""
        s = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M10SelectionSession).where(
                M10SelectionSession.session_no == session_no,
                M10SelectionSession.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if s is None:
            raise BizException("1005013", "选配会话不存在或已过期")
        if s.session_status in ("OPEN", "QUOTED") and s.session_ttl_at \
                and s.session_ttl_at < datetime.now():
            raise BizException("1005013", "选配会话已过期，请重新开始选配")
        return s

    async def open_session(self, payload: SelectionSessionCreateIn,
                           operator: Any, idem_key: Optional[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """打开会话：已有未过期会话则恢复（幂等）。"""
        customer = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M10Customer).where(
                M10Customer.customer_no == payload.customer_no,
                M10Customer.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if customer is None or customer.customer_status == "DISABLED":
            raise BizException("1005001", "顾客不存在或已停用")
        if customer.customer_status == "MERGED":
            raise BizException("1005019", "顾客已合并，请使用目标顾客号 %s"
                               % customer.merged_into_no)

        # 恢复既有会话（幂等）
        existing = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M10SelectionSession).where(
                M10SelectionSession.customer_id == customer.id,
                M10SelectionSession.session_status.in_(["OPEN", "QUOTED"]),
                M10SelectionSession.session_ttl_at > datetime.now(),
                M10SelectionSession.is_deleted.is_(False))
            .order_by(M10SelectionSession.last_active_at.desc())
        )).scalars().first()
        if existing is not None:
            data = await self._session_out(existing, customer, resumed=True)
            return data

        # 并发会话数限制
        salesperson_id = payload.salesperson_id or getattr(operator, "id", None)
        running = (await self.db.execute(
            select(func.count()).select_from(M10SelectionSession).where(
                M10SelectionSession.salesperson_id == salesperson_id,
                M10SelectionSession.session_status.in_(["OPEN", "QUOTED"]),
                M10SelectionSession.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar() or 0
        if running >= MAX_CONCURRENT_SESSIONS:
            raise BizException("1005023",
                               "同时进行的选配会话数已达上限（%d 个），请先结束其他会话"
                               % MAX_CONCURRENT_SESSIONS)

        seq = await redis_client.incr("m10:code:SX:%s" % datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d"))
        session_no = "SX%s%04d" % (datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d"), seq)

        # 取处方与验光快照（上游不可用时降级为空）
        rx_snapshot = None
        rx_no = payload.rx_no
        if rx_no is None and customer.patient_id:
            try:
                rx = await self.rx_client.get_valid_prescription(customer.patient_id)
                rx_no = rx.get("rx_no") if rx else None
                rx_snapshot = rx
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
                rx_snapshot = None
        elif rx_no:
            try:
                rx_snapshot = await self.rx_client.get_prescription(rx_no)
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
                rx_snapshot = None

        now = datetime.now()
        session = M10SelectionSession(
            session_no=session_no,
            customer_id=customer.id,
            customer_no=customer.customer_no,
            session_status="OPEN",
            session_type=payload.session_type,
            rx_no=rx_no,
            optometry_no=payload.optometry_no,
            rx_snapshot=rx_snapshot,
            salesperson_id=salesperson_id,
            store_code=payload.store_code,
            device_id=payload.device_id,
            session_ttl_at=now + timedelta(seconds=SESSION_TTL),
            last_active_at=now,
            opened_at=now,
            created_by=getattr(operator, "id", None),
        )
        self.db.add(session)
        await self.db.commit()

        # 写 Redis 热态
        await redis_client.setex(
            SESSION_KEY % customer.customer_no, SESSION_TTL,
            json.dumps({"session_no": session_no,
                        "customer_no": customer.customer_no,
                        "rx_snapshot": rx_snapshot}, default=str))
        data = await self._session_out(session, customer, resumed=False)
        return data

    async def _session_out(self, s: M10SelectionSession, c: M10Customer,
                           resumed: bool) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """组装会话出参。"""
        return {
            "session_no": s.session_no,
            "customer_no": s.customer_no,
            "session_status": s.session_status,
            "session_type": s.session_type,
            "salesperson_id": s.salesperson_id,
            "salesperson_name": s.salesperson_name,
            "rx_no": s.rx_no,
            "rx_snapshot": s.rx_snapshot,
            "session_ttl_at": s.session_ttl_at,
            "resumed": resumed,
            "opened_at": s.opened_at,
        }

    async def handover(self, session_no: str,
                       payload: SelectionSessionHandoverIn,
                       operator: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """换人接待：更新接待人，草稿零丢失。"""
        s = await self._load_session(session_no)
        if s.session_status not in ("OPEN", "QUOTED"):
            raise BizException("1005014", "会话状态不允许当前操作")
        if s.version != payload.version:
            raise BizException("1005010", "数据已被其他用户修改，请刷新")
        if not payload.reason or len(payload.reason.strip()) < 2:
            raise BizException("40001", "换人接待必须填写原因")

        old_id, old_name = s.salesperson_id, s.salesperson_name
        if s.original_salesperson_id is None:
            s.original_salesperson_id = old_id
        log = s.handler_log or []
        log.append({"from": old_id, "to": payload.new_salesperson_id,
                    "at": datetime.now().isoformat(), "reason": payload.reason})
        s.handler_log = log
        s.salesperson_id = payload.new_salesperson_id
        s.version += 1
        await self.db.commit()

        drafts = (await self.db.execute(
```
